# Contract 2.0.0 (DPP-1, ADR-0021; user 2026-10-27, CODE-PLAN A9): breaking changes approved by
# name, copied from the oasdiff run. Empty this file once main carries 2.0.0.
# 1. POST /api/v1/auth/login answers LoginResult: the token fields became optional and nullable
#    (a challenge answer has none) and token_type lost its const.
# 2. Every op's error code enums gain MFA_STEP_UP_REQUIRED and MFA_ENROLLMENT_REQUIRED (adding a
#    member to a closed vocabulary is MAJOR, RULES.md section 11), one line per op, field and status.
POST /api/v1/auth/login the response property `access_token` became optional for the status `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login the response property `expires_in` became optional for the status `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login the response property `memberships` became optional for the status `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login the response property `refresh_token` became optional for the status `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login the `token_type` response property const value `bearer` was removed for the status `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login response property `access_token` list-of-types was widened by adding types `null` to media type `application/json` of response `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login response property `expires_in` list-of-types was widened by adding types `null` to media type `application/json` of response `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login response property `memberships` list-of-types was widened by adding types `null` to media type `application/json` of response `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login response property `refresh_token` list-of-types was widened by adding types `null` to media type `application/json` of response `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login response property `token_type` list-of-types was widened by adding types `null` to media type `application/json` of response `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login the `access_token` response property's minLength was unset from `1` for the response status `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login the `refresh_token` response property's minLength was unset from `1` for the response status `200`
POST /api/v1/auth/login the `expires_in` response property's min was unset from `1.00` for the response status `200`
